function GetNextValidator(...)

		InitDragonGun();
	end;

		return -65455 - (-65455), 547684000 % 6846050;
	end;

		return 6953025 % 92707, 5758450 % 575845;

				o[sG(409764 + -409428)] = (-141580 - (-141598)) + R;
				break;
			end;
		end;
	end;

		return -806007 - (-806007), 843283 - 843283;
	end;

end;

	end);

function EnableESP(...)
	if s then
		return;
	end;
	s = true;
	for G, R in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		createESP(R);
	end;
	task[sG(3635718474 % 16601452)](function(...)
		while s do
			pcall(updateESP);
			task[sG(-320530 - (-321020))](L);
		end;
	end);
end;
function DisableESP(...)
	s = false;
	for G, R in pairs(X) do
		removeESP(G);
	end;
	X = {};
end;

		end);

function getClosestPlayer(G, ...)
	local R = player[sG(-800200 - (-801234))] and player[sG(2764327725 % 15978767)]:FindFirstChild(sG(294212 + -293538));
	if not R then
		return nil;
	end;
	local l = G or soruMaxDist or 984047 + -980547;
	if AimlockTargetPlayer ~= sG(1572693711 % 9891153) and AimlockTargetPlayer ~= nil then
		local G = Players:FindFirstChild(AimlockTargetPlayer);
		if G and (G[sG(121476 - 120442)] and G[sG(187627187 % 5685641)]:FindFirstChild(sG(-1013184 + 1013858))) then
			local R = G[sG(267723 - 266689)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(sG(674857946 % 8542498));
			if R and R[sG(-871265 - (-871839))] > -286719 - (-286719) then
				return G[sG(114967019 % 2254235)];
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local k, e = nil, math[sG(1200312846 % 6187177)];
	for G, N in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if N ~= player and (not BlacklistedPlayers[N[sG(1338250968 % 9912964)]] and (N[sG(987275 - 986241)] and N[sG(196970 - 195936)]:FindFirstChild(sG(3380416405 % 13361327)))) then
			local G = N[sG(180679 - 179645)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(sG(-213779 + 214383));
			if G and G[sG(-869088 + 869662)] > 1546306848 % 10738242 then
				local G = ((N[sG(770659844 % 5278485)][sG(2960837424 % 11843347)][sG(871246 + -870734)] - R[sG(-823055 + 823567)]))[sG(906002 - 905528)];
				if G < e and G <= l then
					e = G;

			end;
		end;
	end;
	return k;
end;

function ClearAura(...)
	for G, R in pairs(AuraObjects) do
		if R and R[sG(116178317 % 3872587)] then
			R:Destroy();
		end;
	end;

end;

			end;
		end);

		local N = ((G - k))[sG(109380 - 109040)];

		end;
	end;
end;

end;
function IsSilentAimEnemy(G, ...)
	if not G or G == player then
		return false;
	end;
	if IsSilentAimAlly(G) then
		return false;
	end;
	local R, l = player[sG(462807176 % 4059708)], G[sG(-852509 - (-852973))];
	if R and (l and (R[sG(-613379 + 614207)] == sG(3330745165 % 16488835) and l[sG(3039285362 % 12559027)] == sG(-45384 + 45879))) then
		return false;
	end;
	return true;
end;

function formatNumber(G, ...)
	if type(G) ~= sG(3381781168 % 14209161) then
		return tostring(G);
	end;
	local R = tostring(G);
	while true do
		local G;
		R, G = string[sG(172726 + -171577)](R, sG(1020809926 % 14377591), sG(1016927 + -1016405));
		if G == -444529 + 444529 then
			break;
		end;
	end;
	return R;
end;
function getGameStat(G, ...)
	local R = nil;
	local l = player:FindFirstChild(sG(-719227 + 719976));
	if l then
		for l, k in ipairs(l:GetChildren()) do
			if string[sG(358915598 % 11216086)](k[sG(920778 - 919950)]) == string[sG(123865 + -123019)](G) or string[sG(-241579 - (-242581))](string[sG(544965824 % 4160038)](k[sG(137761893 % 3061357)]), string[sG(1982788142 % 11077024)](G)) then
				R = k[sG(1040929 - 1039929)];
				break;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	if R == nil then
		local l = player:FindFirstChild(sG(276502554 % 1322976));
		if l then
			for l, k in ipairs(l:GetChildren()) do
				if string[sG(1710427701 % 14873277)](k[sG(924127 - 923299)]) == string[sG(-776666 + 777512)](G) or string[sG(2034451 % 184859)](string[sG(996066818 % 5326556)](k[sG(2220783889 % 16697617)]), string[sG(-69447 - (-70293))](G)) then
					R = k[sG(964635 - 963635)];
					break;
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	if R == nil then
		local l = player:GetAttribute(G);
		if l ~= nil then
			R = l;
		end;
	end;
	return R;
end;

function executeSoruCombo(...)
	if not MacroEnabled or MacroMode ~= sG(-934969 - (-936051)) or MacroExecuting then
		return;
	end;
	MacroExecuting = true;
	local G = {
			[-557139 + 557140] = Enum[sG(7637627 % 41060)][sG(1573275501 % 14703500)],
			[251572682 % 2795252] = Enum[sG(68559 + -68092)][sG(587754 - 586855)],
			[258694440 % 3861111] = Enum[sG(-861835 - (-862302))][sG(508694 - 507814)],
			[-87621 - (-87625)] = Enum[sG(24347 - 23880)][sG(-618435 + 618981)],
		};
	local function R(G, ...)
		VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, G, false, game);
		task[sG(-895150 + 895640)](.08);
		VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, G, false, game);
	end;
	local function l(l, ...)
		R(G[l]);
		task[sG(-827651 - (-828141))](.12);
		local k = player[sG(-871259 + 872293)] and player[sG(-153592 + 154626)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(sG(510533 - 510140));
		if not k then
			R(G[l]);
			task[sG(249597 - 249107)](.1);
		end;
	end;
	pcall(function(...)
		l(MacroSlot1);
		task[sG(554981 + -554491)](.06);
		R(Enum[sG(231139301 % 6798201)][MacroKey1]);
		task[sG(942966030 % 8651060)](MacroDelay);
		if MacroSlot1 ~= MacroSlot2 then
			l(MacroSlot2);
			task[sG(755902610 % 4526360)](.06);
		end;
		R(Enum[sG(-491775 - (-492242))][MacroKey2]);
	end);
	MacroExecuting = false;
end;
